Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The linear variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 995 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 812 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the linear decomposition. By minimality, the element a^995 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 812-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
